- **deterministic-motion-render**: Build or audit a code-driven motion-graphics / video renderer (HTML+CSS+JS scenes captured frame by frame with headless Chrome and ffmpeg) so output is reproducible. Use when AI-written animation code renders differently on re-runs, when jumping to a frame gives a different image than playing up to it, when an agent writes scene code that must not touch clocks/randomness/network, or when you want sound as data (cue lists) instead of audio code. ([Kein-chronicle/deterministic-html-motion-render](https://github.com/Kein-chronicle/deterministic-html-motion-render/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **deterministic-motion-rendering**: Provides offline animation and video export guidance when rendering scenes frame by frame, seeking arbitrary frames, or verifying reproducible fixed-timeline media. Covers scene readiness, frame evaluation and exported media evidence rather than ordinary live UI interaction. ([dandgabr/Coacus](https://github.com/dandgabr/Coacus/tree/HEAD/knowledge/skills/domains/design/deterministic-motion-rendering) · ★10)
- **deterministic-motion-screenshot-bench**: Build deterministic frozen-time screenshot benchmarks for animated web apps (CSS/WAAPI + rAF + GSAP): virtual clock injection, population-stable animation pinning, virtual-frame gesture spacing, double-capture nondeterminism check. ([fpcMotif/nix-darwin](https://github.com/fpcMotif/nix-darwin/tree/HEAD/modules/home/skills/personal/deterministic-motion-screenshot-bench))
